# All Sourced Roles v3 — Chad Butler (649 unique roles)

Compiled 2026-08-03 · 15 waves · ~100 agents · 800+ companies. Title-insurance companies removed per instruction; exact cross-wave duplicates collapsed (first occurrence kept).

**NONE of these was verified by loading a live posting** — the sourcing environment's network blocked every job board; all detail is from search-index copies (source named per entry). Postings may have closed. Re-read degree/license lines on the live page before applying. **⚠️ = flagged by the adversarial audit** (possible fabrication or rule concern) — verify those first or skip. Most remote roles do NOT confirm Utah eligibility explicitly; the ★ CRITERIA-FIRST section leads with roles where Utah IS named.

Screened for: no securities licenses · remote (Utah-eligible) or Utah/Mountain West/national field territory · no office/hybrid anywhere incl. Utah · no Utah-HQ employers · no title companies · 5+ yrs · no SDR/BDR/MSL/phone-queue · no Merck (incl. Verona Pharma) or Human Interest · sales and non-sales; quota flagged where captured.

Full audit trail (767 documented exclusions with quoted disqualifiers, 985 unverified leads): sourced_roles_2026-08-03.md on branch claude/pharma-sales-job-search-fqmkqd.
